# Claims ledger — every number in `docs/reviews` and `docs/research`

Generated by `port/scripts/claims_ledger.py` on **2026-11-07**. Commit `2e7b85e`; docs root `/home/sehaxe/dormouse/docs`; artifact root `/home/sehaxe/dormouse`.

**2199 claims** in 156 documents — **16 derived**, 2172 unverifiable, 11 retracted; 6 derived values differ from the stated one by more than the noise floor.

| total | derived | unverifiable | retracted | mismatches |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 2199 | 16 | 2172 | 11 | 6 |

## How to read the risk list (known limitation, stated up front)

Pairing a claim to a log line is heuristic: the checker takes the eval line at the
step the sentence names, and when the sentence names no step it takes the nearest
logged step. **A mismatch is therefore a pointer for a human, not a proof.** The
top rows below illustrate the failure mode: `muon-audit.md:12` states `2.1867`
and the checker derived `1.1e-05` — that is it reading an LR value, not a BPB.
Six of the 2 199 claims were mechanically re-derivable at all; the other 2 172
carry no artifact the checker can read, and the honest reading of that number is
"our documents mostly do not name a machine-checkable artifact yet".

What the run *does* prove, and is worth acting on: 11 claims are retracted by the
§3.2 / broken-Muon rules, and the gate (`--check`) now refuses a NEW number that
names no evidence at all.

## Method

Four shapes are extracted, inline and from table columns: `X BPB`, `N params`, `Y× faster`/`s/step`, `±Z`. Every claim records its `file:line`, value, unit, kind and the evidence the document itself names. Kinds: **measured** (a log, checkpoint or bench file is named), **computed** (arithmetic, a config or a commit is named), **cited-paper** (an arXiv id or a paper is named), **speculation** (nothing is named).

Verification is mechanical and deliberately narrow:

* **BPB** — `bpb = nats / bytes / ln 2`, recomputed from the eval line at the claimed step (JSONL `loss`/`fin` and `scored`; text logs' `ce=`/`bpb=`/`over N B`). Floor **0.043 BPB**.
* **params** — rebuilt from the config or the checkpoint's own `.config.toml` by the repo's counter (`dormouse info`'s `param_count`, meta device). Floor **2%**.
* **speed** — `tokens_per_step / median(tokens_per_s)` from the JSONL run, or a ratio of two named runs. Floor **10%**.
* **±** — a noise estimate; only its absence can be checked.

Status is `DERIVED` (recomputed, with the delta shown), `UNVERIFIABLE` (no artifact that can re-derive it — the missing artifact is named), or `RETRACTED` (§3.2 or a scoping audit says the number measured something else). A derived mismatch is still `DERIVED`; it goes on the risk list.

### Retraction rules applied

| rule | what it catches | authority |
|---|---|---|
| in-VRAM Engram eval defect | any claim whose evidence is `engram_ce` or `small12`, or a config with `use_engram=true` and `engram_ram=false` | AGENTS.md §3.2, fix `7adda92` |
| frozen attention arm | `kda_ce`, `kda_full`, `kda_smoke{,2..5}`, `noengram_ce`, `probe2` | AGENTS.md §3.2, `8fa5d4c` |
| broken Muon | a `s/step` or BPB claim from an `opt=mix` run logged before 2026-11-07 (`muon_lr_mult` overwritten every step) | `docs/reviews/2026-11-07-muon-audit.md` D1-D2 |
| headline vs fin | a logged `bpb` cited as the model's BPB when `|bpb - bpb_fin| > 0.043` and the line does not say headline/depth-avg | `docs/reviews/2026-11-06-bpb-accounting.md` |
| retracted literals | 6.453 BPB (`engram_ce`), 7.5M/12.2M params, 1.7-2.0× and 11.2× fused, 956/188 ms `--no-kda`, 8303/10809 ms optimizer, GR -0.026 | AGENTS.md §3.2 |

## Gate

A claim is **new** when its document's date (the `YYYY-MM-DD` filename prefix) is later than `--today`. A new claim fails the gate when it is `UNVERIFIABLE` **and names no artifact at all** (no log, checkpoint, config, commit or paper): a new number must name its evidence, per ADR-0018 rule 1. An `UNVERIFIABLE` claim that names an artifact the ledger cannot read passes — that is an absence on record, not a silent one.

**PASS** — no claim dated after 2026-11-07 is unverifiable without a named artifact.
